# TrustBank CBS — Legacy Report Inventory (xnet)

Every report in the legacy xnet app, traced **`.rpt` layout → stored procedure → screen → menu/module**.

> **Generated:** 2026-09-23 by [scripts/legacy-report-inventory.py](../../scripts/legacy-report-inventory.py) — re-run to refresh; do not hand-edit.  
> **Source scanned:** `E:\adtemp\_del_now\20260904_cbs_source_code_xnet\Trust.Bank.Publish` (code + layouts); extra layouts from `E:\adtemp\_del_now\20260904_TrustBank.XNETT_ANI_COPY\Trust.Bank.Publish` (`.rpt` only, its code is not scanned). Analysis only.  
> **Machine-readable:** `reports-by-rpt.tsv` · `reports-by-menu.tsv`. Module = the menu's module (`menus.tsv`), as in [README.md](README.md).

## 1. How legacy reporting works

- **Engine:** SAP Crystal Reports (assemblies 10.2.3600 in `Web.Config`, image handler 13.0.2000). One shared viewer, `Reports/CrystalViewer.aspx`, shows every report.
- **Push model:** the screen runs a stored procedure, packs the DataSet into `ReportAttributes`, and the viewer calls `ReportDocument.Load(rpt)` + `SetDataSource(ds)` (sub-reports too). The `.rpt` never connects to the DB — so **the data side carries over as-is; only the layout needs a new engine.**
- **Dot-matrix twins:** many reports ship a `…DMP.rpt` layout for DMP printers next to the laser one.
- **Other outputs:** DataSet → Excel (`application/vnd.ms-excel`), ActiveX DOS printing (`dosprint.aspx`, IE only), DOCX templates (`Ad.Docx`), charts (`dotnetCHARTING`).
- **Dispatcher screens:** a few big screens (`ReportOptions.aspx`, `ReportSettings.aspx`, `SeedReportOptions.aspx`) serve most menus via `Select Case Request("Source")`. Each menu is resolved through its own `Case` branch and the Subs it calls (`Match = case`); where the report is picked at runtime (dropdown, `Source` -> number) the whole screen is listed (`Match = screen`).
- **Procedure tracing:** procs are taken from the Sub/Function that names the `.rpt` (`ProcScope = block`); when that block calls none they come from the whole file (`file`, less precise). Procs reached only through the business-logic DLL are not visible.
